run = <app.modules.marketdemandanalyst.models.MarketAnalysisRun object at 0xffff3777bb90>

    @staticmethod
    def review_from_run(run: MarketAnalysisRun) -> SkillReview:
        manifest = run.candidate_manifest
        records = []
        for record in manifest.get("reconciliations", []):
            records.append(
                {
                    **{
                        key: record[key]
                        for key in (
                            "reconciliation_id",
                            "candidate_id",
                            "previous_candidate_ids",
                            "reason",
                            "previous_selected_candidate_ids",
                            "previous_excluded_candidate_ids",
                            "resolved",
                        )
                        if key in record
                    },
                    "previous_candidates": [
                        {
                            "candidate_id": item["candidate_id"],
                            "display_name": item.get("display_name")
                            or "Unknown candidate",
                        }
                        for item in record.get("historical_candidates", [])
                    ],
                }
            )
        omissions = available_evidence_omissions(run)
        return SkillReview(
            run_id=run.id,
>           analysis_metadata=AnalysisMetadata(
                country=run.config.get("country"),
                job_sources=run.config.get("sources") or [],
                evidence_sources=run.config.get("evidence_sources") or [],

                occupations=run.config.get("selected_onet_occupations") or [],
                created_at=run.created_at,
                updated_at=run.updated_at,
            ),
            candidate_revision=run.candidate_revision,
            selection_revision=run.selection_revision,
            categories=manifest.get("categories", []),
            candidates=manifest.get("candidates", []),
            selected_candidate_ids=run.selected_candidate_ids,
            excluded_candidate_ids=run.excluded_candidate_ids,
            recommendations=run.recommendations,
            reconciliations=records,
            source_outcomes=run.source_outcomes,
            extraction_coverage={
                **deepcopy(run.extraction_coverage),
                "acknowledged": manifest.get("acknowledged_omissions", []) == omissions,
            },
            integration_review={

                for key, value in run.integration_review.items()
                if not key.startswith("_")
            }
            if run.integration_review
            else None,

node.description = concept.description\n    MERGE (skill)-[:REQUIRES_CONCEPT]->(node)\n)\nRETURN reference.id AS id\n"

(skill)-[mapping:MAPPED_TO]->(chapter)\n    SET mapping.current_for_new_selection = CASE WHEN row.target_kind = 'career'\n        THEN row.available ELSE mapping.current_for_new_selection END\n)\nMERGE (reference:COURSE_SKILL_REFERENCE {id: row.reference_id})\nON CREATE SET reference.course_id = row.course_id, reference.target_identity = row.target_identity\nWITH course, row, skill, reference\nWHERE reference.course_id = row.course_id AND reference.target_identity = row.target_identity\nSET reference.available_for_new_selection = row.available, reference.approved_revision = row.approved_revision,\n    reference.projection_revision = row.approved_revision, reference.source = row.source, reference.origin = row.origin\nMERGE (course)-[:HAS_COURSE_SKILL_REFERENCE]->(reference)\nMERGE (reference)-[:FOR_SKILL]->(skill)\nFOREACH (concept IN row.concepts |\n    MERGE (node:CONCEPT {name: concept.name})\n    ON CREATE SET node.description = concept.description\n    MERGE (skill)-[:REQUIRES_CONCEPT]->(node)\n)\nRETURN reference.id AS id\n"
